.. _X(75):

X(75) = ISOTOMIC CONJUGATE OF INCENTER
======================================

.. figure:: x-00075.svg
   :alt: X(75) = ISOTOMIC CONJUGATE OF INCENTER
   :align: center

   **X(75) = ISOTOMIC CONJUGATE OF INCENTER** constructed on acute reference triangle :math:`(a, b, c) = (5, 6, 7)` with unit baseline :math:`AB = 1`.

Coordinates
-----------

Trilinears
~~~~~~~~~~

* :math:`1/a^{2} : 1/b^{2} : 1/c^{2}`
* :math:`1/(1 - \cos 2A) : 1/(1 - \cos 2B) : 1/(1 - \cos 2C)`
* :math:`S_{A} + S^{2} : S_{B} + S^{2} : S_{C} + S^{2}`
* :math:`\sec^{2}(A/2) + \csc^{2}(A/2) : :`
* :math:`d(a,b,c) : :`, where d(a,b,c) = distance from A to Lemoine axis
* :math:`h(a,b,c) : :`, where h(a,b,c) = (distance from A to antiorthic axis)\ :sup:`2`

Barycentrics
~~~~~~~~~~~~

* :math:`1/a : 1/b : 1/c`
* :math:`\csc A : \csc B : \csc C`

* **Kimberling (6, 9, 13) Search Value:** :math:`3.706459623147`

Linear Combinations
-------------------

* :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>` = (csc A)\*[A] + (csc B)\*[B] + (csc C)\*[C], where A, B, C denote angles and [A], [B], [C] denote vertices
* :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>` = 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` + X(1278), 6 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - X(3644), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 4 X(3739), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 4 X(4681), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` + 2 X(4686), 6 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 5 X(4687), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 8 X(4698), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 5 X(4699), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 5 X(4704), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 2 X(4718), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` + 4 X(4726), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 8 X(4739), 6 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 7 X(4751), 5 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 4 X(4755), 6 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` + X(4764), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 7 X(4772), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - X(4788), 3 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` + 5 X(4821), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 7 X(27268), 9 :ref:`X(2) <X(2)>` - 10 X(31238), 2 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` + X(1278), 4 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - X(3644), 4 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 3 X(4664), 3 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 2 X(4681), 4 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 5 X(4687), :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 3 X(4688), 3 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 4 X(4698), 2 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 5 X(4699), 6 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 5 X(4704), 3 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - X(4718), :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` + 2 X(4726), :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 4 X(4739), 2 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` + 3 X(4740), 4 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 7 X(4751), 5 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 6 X(4755), 4 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` + X(4764), 2 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 7 X(4772), 6 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - X(4788), 2 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` + 5 X(4821), 6 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 7 X(27268), 3 :ref:`X(37) <X(37)>` - 5 X(31238), 4 X(142) - 3 X(27475), X(144) - 3 X(27484), X(192) - 4 X(3739), 2 X(192) - 3 X(4664), 3 X(192) - 4 X(4681), X(192) + 2 X(4686), 2 X(192) - 5 X(4687), X(192) - 6 X(4688), 3 X(192) - 8 X(4698), X(192) - 5 X(4699), 3 X(192) - 5 X(4704), 3 X(192) - 2 X(4718), X(192) + 4 X(4726), X(192) - 8 X(4739), X(192) + 3 X(4740), 2 X(192) - 7 X(4751), 5 X(192) - 12 X(4755), 2 X(192) + X(4764), X(192) - 7 X(4772)

Notes
-----

.. container:: note-item

   Let A2B2C2 be the 2nd Conway triangle. Let A' be the :term:`trilinear pole <trilinear-pole>` of line B2C2, and define B' and C' cyclically. The lines AA', BB', CC' concur in :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>`. (:person:`Randy Hutson <randy-hutson>`, December 10, 2016)

.. container:: note-item

   Let A4B4C4 be the 4th Conway triangle. Let A' be the trilinear pole of line B4C4, and define B' and C' cyclically. The lines AA', BB', CC' concur in :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>`. (Randy Hutson, December 10, 2016)

.. container:: note-item

   Let AaBaCa, AbBbCb, AcBcCc be the A-, B-, and C-anti-altimedial triangles, resp. Let A' be the trilinear product Ba\*Ca, and define B' and C' cyclically. The lines AA', BB', CC' concur in :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>`. (Randy Hutson, November 2, 2017)

.. container:: note-item

   Let A\ :sub:`23`\ B\ :sub:`23`\ C\ :sub:`23` be Gemini triangle 23. Let A' be the perspector of conic {A,B,C,B\ :sub:`23`,C\ :sub:`23`}}, and define B' and C' cyclically. The lines AA', BB', CC' concur in :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>`. (Randy Hutson, January 15, 2019)

.. container:: note-item

   Let A\ :sub:`40`\ B\ :sub:`40`\ C\ :sub:`40` be Gemini triangle 40. Let A' be the perspector of conic {A,B,C,B\ :sub:`40`,C\ :sub:`40`}}, and define B' and C' cyclically. The lines AA', BB', CC' concur in :ref:`X(75) <X(75)>`. (Randy Hutson, January 15, 2019)
